In January 2007, Zuffa and video game developer/publisher [THQ](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/THQ) announced a license agreement giving THQ worldwide rights to develop titles under the UFC brand. The agreement gives THQ exclusive rights to current and next-generation consoles as well as to PC and handheld titles. Also included are "certain wireless rights" which were not detailed. The licensing agreement was set to expire in 2011, although it appeared to have been extended to 2017. On June 4, 2012, THQ announced they will be giving the license of UFC Undisputed to EA.[^cite_note-534]

### Action figures

#### Round 5

The first UFC action figure collectibles were released by Round 5 Corporation in May 2008.[^cite_note-535] Series one of their figures includes [Quinton "Rampage" Jackson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Quinton_%22Rampage%22_Jackson), [Matt Hughes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Matt_Hughes_\(fighter\)), [Tito Ortiz](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tito_Ortiz), and [Randy Couture](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Randy_Couture). Series two (released on November 10, 2008) includes [Wanderlei Silva](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wanderlei_Silva), [Sean Sherk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sean_Sherk), [Rich Franklin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rich_Franklin), and [Anderson Silva](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anderson_Silva).[^cite_note-536]

In July 2009, Round 5 acquired the UFC license through Jakks Pacific and subsequently released five more series under the UFC and Pride brands. Two packs were released in August 2010 and include a UFC Octagon cage and Pride ring display stand. Limited edition versions include fabric walk-out tees or paint variations and are limited in number with foil and holographic packaging variances. Special edition and exclusive versions have been released at various UFC Fan Expo events.[^cite_note-537]

#### Jakks Pacific

On June 10, 2008, it was announced that UFC had signed an exclusive four-year contract with [Jakks Pacific](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jakks_Pacific) to create action figures for UFC. As of 2009 the schedule envisages the release of these figures in November 2009. They have currently been 8 series released and they feature special Legends, Pride, and WEC style figures as well. Three 2 packs series have also been released, as well as several expo and internet exclusives. There are also several different octagon cage playsets that have been released, including the "Octagon Playset", "Official Scale Octagon Playset", and "Electronic Reaction Octagon Playset". A Pridestyle ring playset was also originally planned; however, no news have been given on its status or release date since then.[^cite_note-538][^cite_note-539][^cite_note-540]

Jakks Pacific UFC Deluxe Figure Lineups

#### Jazwares

In 2020, the UFC and toy company [Jazwares](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jazwares) entered into an agreement to release a new line of UFC action figures.[^cite_note-545] The first wave of Jazwares' UFC figures were scheduled to be released in Fall 2020.

### DVDs

Various UFC events have been released onto DVD. [UFC 23](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UFC_23) through [UFC 29](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UFC_29) were not released in the US on home video or DVD by SEG. They have since been released onto boxsets which feature around 10 events each set, in chronological order.[^cite_note-546]

### PlayStation Network and Xbox Live

UFC on-demand content launched for [Xbox 360](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Xbox_360) on December 20, 2011. Subscribers were able to view pay-per-view events in high definition, connect with friends to predict fight results, and have the ability to compare fighter statistics and records.[^cite_note-547] The [UFC Fight Pass](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UFC_Fight_Pass) application was also planned for [PlayStation 4](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/PlayStation_4) in early 2015.[^cite_note-548] In 2015, UFC Fight Pass became available on the Xbox One.[^cite_note-549] In December 2022, a PlayStation version of the app was released.

### NFTs

In August 2021, the UFC began to release NFTs to commemorate big fights, milestones and moments via their official trading card partner, [Panini America](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Panini_America).[^cite_note-550]

## International broadcasters

The UFC's PPV events are broadcast live on [pay-per-view](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ESPN+) in the US and [TNT Sports Box Office](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/TNT_Sports_Box_Office) in the United Kingdom.

### United Kingdom

In the UK; BT Sport's first ever live event was August 3, 2013 [UFC 163](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UFC_163) headlined by [José Aldo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jos%C3%A9_Aldo) and [The Korean Zombie](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Korean_Zombie).[^cite_note-551] BT Sport were the first major broadcaster in the UK for the UFC where previously they had small coverage on [Bravo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bravo_\(British_TV_channel\)), [Setanta Sports](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Setanta_Sports)[^cite_note-552] and finally [ESPN UK](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ESPN_in_the_United_Kingdom) in August, 2012[^cite_note-553] until the BT Sport deal was in place.

As of August 2025, TNT Sports, the successor of BT Sport, holds exclusive rights to UFC in the United Kingdom.

### Mexico

Events are broadcast live on [Fighting Sports Network](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Televisa) in Pay TV (Cable and Satellite) of SKY Satellite. Free TV Channels in Mexico, [Fox Sports](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fox_Sports_\(Latin_American_TV_network\)) and [Fox Premium](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fox_Premium) in Pay TV (Cable and Satellite) Central America are broadcast with tape-delay.

### Brazil

In Brazil, events are broadcast live on [open television](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Free-to-air) through [Rede Bandeirantes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rede_Bandeirantes), which transmits the Fight Night events and preliminary cards of main events, while the numbered events are on [Fight Pass](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UFC_Fight_Pass).\[^cite_note-[www.uol.com.br-554](http://www.uol.com.br-554/)] Previously, [Rede Globo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rede_Globo) broadcast it on its own martial arts channel [Combate](https://pt.wikipedia.org/wiki/Combate%20\(canal%20de%20televis%C3%A3o\)) from 2011 to 2022, with select events on its broadcast network.\[^cite_note-[www.uol.com.br-554](http://www.uol.com.br-554/)]

### Asia

In Southeast Asia (exclude the Philippines), Hong Kong, and Papua New Guinea, UFC events are broadcast on [Fox Movies Premium](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fox_Movies_Premium) (2012–2013)[^cite_note-555] and [Fox Sports](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fox_Sports_Asia)[^cite_note-556] (2013–2021).[^cite_note-557]

In [India](14533.md), events are broadcast on [Sony Ten 2](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sony_Ten), Sony Ten 1, Sony Ten 1 HD and Sony Ten 2 HD.[^cite_note-558]

In the [Philippines](23440.md), UFC was also aired on [Balls](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Balls_\(TV_channel\)) (now [ABS-CBN Sports + Action HD](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ABS-CBN_Sports_and_Action)) from 2009 until 2015, since moved to [Sports5](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sports5) (including [TV5](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_5_Network), [AksyonTV](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/AksyonTV) and [Hyper](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hyper_\(TV_channel\)) on Channel 91 (SD) and 261 (HD) via [Cignal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cignal)) starting January 3, 2016 until December 31, 2018, and is currently airing on [TAP Digital Media Ventures Corporation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/TAP_Digital_Media_Ventures_Corporation)-owned sports cable television channel [Premier Sports](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Premier_Sports_\(Philippine_TV_channel\)) since October 2021.[^cite_note-559][^cite_note-560][^cite_note-561][^cite_note-562]

In [Sri Lanka](26750.md), UFC events have been broadcast on several television networks over the years. Previously, UFC events were aired on various channels from 2012 to 2017, including [Dialog TV](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dialog_TV) and [PEO TV](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Peo_TV).[^cite_note-MMAJunkieMNC-563][^cite_note-SindoUFC-564] Starting January 15, 2018, [Sony Sports Ten 2](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sony_Sports_Ten_2?action=edit\&redlink=1) became the primary broadcaster for UFC events in Sri Lanka, airing the events in English.[^cite_note-565] Additionally, UFC events have been available via [UFC Fight Pass](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UFC_Fight_Pass), which offers live streaming of events and on-demand content for viewers in Sri Lanka.[^cite_note-566]

In [Indonesia](14579.md), UFC events are currently broadcast on [tvOne](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/TvOne_\(Indonesia\)) starting January 15, 2018;[^cite_note-567] previously, this events was also broadcast on [RCTI](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/RCTI),[^cite_note-568] [iNews](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/INews)[^cite_note-SindoUFC-564] since 2012 until the end of 2017.[^cite_note-MMAJunkieMNC-563]

### United Arab Emirates

On April 13, 2019, UFC's event was broadcast on [Abu Dhabi Media](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Abu_Dhabi_Media).[^cite_note-569]

### Poland

In Poland since 2018 the official broadcaster of UFC it's [Polsat Sport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Polsat_Sport).[^cite_note-570] Previously the UFC events in this nation are broadcast by [Orange Sport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Orange_Sport_\(Poland\)) and [Extreme Sports Channel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Extreme_Sports_Channel). The main Polish broadcasters of UFC telecasts, are Andrzej Janisz and Łukasz "Juras" Jurkowski.

### Canada

In 2024, [Rogers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rogers_Sports_&_Media) successfully bid for the rights to broadcast UFC in Canada on its [Sportsnet](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sportsnet) channels. The UFC was previously on Sportsnet and French broadcaster [TVA](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/TVA_Sports) from September 2011 through the end of 2014 in Canada.[^cite_note-571]

### Russia

In Russian, UFC events are broadcast on Match TV, a Russian sports channel owned by Gazprom Media, starting 2022.[^cite_note-572]

[^cite_note-60]: &#x20;Staff Writer. ["Mixed martial arts: Coleman returns; he just can't use his head butt"](https://www.dispatch.com/story/sports/college/2009/01/15/mixed-martial-arts-coleman-returns/23363365007/). *The Columbus Dispatch*. Retrieved March 10, 2024. Then there were the rules changes, including no more head-butting, which negatively affected Coleman's talents. Wrestlers once dominated the sport, because they would put opponents on the ground and pound them with hands and elbows. But as limitations on permissible striking areas increased in the late 1990s, the effectiveness of Coleman's "ground and pound" techniques decreased.\
    \
    Suddenly, it wasn't as easy to "take a guy to the ground and pound the (bleep) out of him until he quits," he said.\
    \
    "When they took out the head butt, it made the jujitsu game (a form of ground fighting) more effective. It completely changed the sport so for a while there, I was a little bit lost. As fast as I climbed to the top, I fell to the bottom just as fast."
